<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Mujh se Muhammad bin Muthanna ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Abdul Wahab Saqafi ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Ayyub Sakhtiyani ne bayan kiya, un se Muhammad bin Sireen ne, un se Abdur Rahman bin Abi Bakra (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ne aur un se Abu Bakra (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke zamana apni asal halat par ghoom kar aa gaya hai. Us din ki tarah jab Allah ne zameen-o-aasman ko paida kiya tha. Dekho! Saal ke barah mahine hote hain. Chaar in mein se hurmat wale mahine hain. Teen lagatar hain, Zil-Qadah, Zil-Hajj aur Muharram (aur chautha) Rajab-e-Mudar jo Jamadi-ul-Awwal aur Shaban ke beech mein padta hai. (Phir aap ne daryaft farmaya) Yeh kaun sa mahina hai? Hum ne kaha ke Allah aur un ke Rasool ko behtar ilm hai. Is par aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) khamosh ho gaye. Hum ne samjha shayad aap mashhoor naam ke siwa is ka koi aur naam rakhenge. Lekin aap ne farmaya: Kya yeh Zil-Hajj nahi hai? Hum bole ke kyun nahi. Phir daryaft farmaya aur yeh shehar kaun sa hai? Hum bole ke Allah aur un ke Rasool ko zyada behtar ilm hai. Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) khamosh ho gaye. Hum ne samjha shayad is ka koi aur naam aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) rakhenge, jo mashhoor naam ke alawa hoga, lekin aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kya yeh Makkah nahi hai? Hum bole kyun nahi (yeh Makkah hi hai). Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne daryaft farmaya aur yeh din kaun sa hai? Hum bole ke Allah aur un ke Rasool ko zyada behtar ilm hai, phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) khamosh ho gaye aur hum ne samjha shayad is ka aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) is ke mashhoor naam ke siwa koi aur naam rakhenge, lekin aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kya yeh Yaum-un-Nahr (qurbani ka din) nahi hai? Hum bole ke kyun nahi. Is ke baad aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Pas tumhara khoon aur tumhara maal. Muhammad ne bayan kiya ke mera khayal hai ke Abu Bakra (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne yeh bhi kaha, aur tumhari izzat tum par isi tarah haram hai jis tarah yeh din ka tumhare is shehar aur tumhare is mahine mein aur tum bahut jald apne Rabb se miloge aur woh tum se tumhare aamal ke baare mein sawal karega. Haan, pas mere baad tum gumrah na ho jana ke ek dusre ki gardan maarne lago. Haan aur jo yahan maujood hain woh un logon ko pahuncha dein jo maujood nahi hain, ho sakta hai ke jise woh pahunchayein un mein se koi aisa bhi ho jo yahan baaz sunne walon se zyada is (hadith) ko yaad rakh sakta ho. Muhammad bin Sireen jab is hadith ka zikr karte toh farmate ke Muhammad (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne sach farmaya. Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke toh kya main ne pahuncha diya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne do martaba yeh jumla farmaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4406]</p>

## Narration 264

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4410)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Military Expeditions led by the Prophet (pbuh) (Al-Maghaazi)</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ الْمُنْذِرِ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو ضَمْرَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا مُوسَى بْنُ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ ابْنَ عُمَرَ ـ رضى الله عنهما ـ أَخْبَرَهُمْ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَلَقَ رَأْسَهُ فِي حَجَّةِ الْوَدَاعِ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4410]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मुझ से इब्राहिम बिन मुन्ज़िर ख़ुज़ामी ने बयान किया, उन्होंने कहा हम से अबू ज़मरा अनस बिन अयाज़ ने बयान किया, उन्होंने कहा हम से मूसा बिन उक़बा ने बयान किया, उन से नाफ़े ने और उन्हें अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने ख़बर दी के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने हज्जतुल विदा में अपना सर मुंडवाया था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4410]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مجھ سے ابراہیم بن منذر خزامی نے بیان کیا، انہوں نے کہا ہم سے ابوضمرہ انس بن عیاض نے بیان کیا، انہوں نے کہا ہم سے موسیٰ بن عقبہ نے بیان کیا، ان سے نافع نے اور انہیں عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما نے خبر دی کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے حجۃ الوداع میں اپنا سر منڈوایا تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4410]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 265

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4411)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Military Expeditions led by the Prophet (pbuh) (Al-Maghaazi)</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَكْرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي مُوسَى بْنُ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَخْبَرَهُ ابْنُ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَلَقَ فِي حَجَّةِ الْوَدَاعِ وَأُنَاسٌ مِنْ أَصْحَابِهِ وَقَصَّرَ بَعْضُهُمْ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Ibn `Umar: During Hajjat-ul-Wada`, the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and some of his companions got their heads shaved while some of his companions got their head-hair cut short</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से उबैदुल्लाह बिन सईद ने बयान किया, कहा हम से मुहम्मद बिन बक्र ने बयान किया, कहा हम से इब्ने-जुरैज ने बयान किया, उन से मूसा बिन उक़बा ने, उन्हें नाफ़े ने और उन्हें इब्ने-उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने ख़बर दी के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) और आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ आप के बाज़ असहाब ने हज्जतुल विदा के मौक़े पर सर मुंडवाया था और बाज़ दूसरे सहाबा (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हुम) ने सिर्फ़ तराशवा लिया था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے عبیداللہ بن سعید نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے محمد بن بکر نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے ابن جریج نے بیان کیا، ان سے موسیٰ بن عقبہ نے، انہیں نافع نے اور انہیں ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما نے خبر دی کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اور آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ آپ کے بعض اصحاب نے حجۃ الوداع کے موقع پر سر منڈوایا تھا اور بعض دوسرے صحابہ رضی اللہ عنہم نے صرف ترشوا لیا تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Ubaidullah bin Saeed ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Muhammad bin Bakr ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Ibn-e-Jurayj ne bayan kiya, un se Musa bin Uqba ne, unhein Nafi ne aur unhein Ibn-e-Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ne khabar di ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aur aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath aap ke baaz as-haab ne Hajjatul Wida ke mauqe par sar mundwaya tha aur baaz dusre sahaba (Radi Allahu Anhum) ne sirf tarashwa liya tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4411]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Military Expeditions led by the Prophet (pbuh) (Al-Maghaazi)</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْعَلاَءِ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو أُسَامَةَ، عَنْ بُرَيْدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بُرْدَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي بُرْدَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي مُوسَى ـ رضى الله عنه ـ قَالَ أَرْسَلَنِي أَصْحَابِي إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَسْأَلُهُ الْحُمْلاَنَ لَهُمْ، إِذْ هُمْ مَعَهُ فِي جَيْشِ الْعُسْرَةِ وَهْىَ غَزْوَةُ تَبُوكَ فَقُلْتُ يَا نَبِيَّ اللَّهِ، إِنَّ أَصْحَابِي أَرْسَلُونِي إِلَيْكَ لِتَحْمِلَهُمْ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ وَاللَّهِ لاَ أَحْمِلُكُمْ عَلَى شَىْءٍ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ وَوَافَقْتُهُ، وَهْوَ غَضْبَانُ وَلاَ أَشْعُرُ، وَرَجَعْتُ حَزِينًا مِنْ مَنْعِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم، وَمِنْ مَخَافَةِ أَنْ يَكُونَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَجَدَ فِي نَفْسِهِ عَلَىَّ، فَرَجَعْتُ إِلَى أَصْحَابِي فَأَخْبَرْتُهُمُ الَّذِي قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم، فَلَمْ أَلْبَثْ إِلاَّ سُوَيْعَةً إِذْ سَمِعْتُ بِلاَلاً يُنَادِي أَىْ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ قَيْسٍ‏.‏ فَأَجَبْتُهُ، فَقَالَ أَجِبْ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَدْعُوكَ، فَلَمَّا أَتَيْتُهُ، قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ خُذْ هَذَيْنِ الْقَرِينَيْنِ ـ وَهَذَيْنِ الْقَرِينَيْنِ لِسِتَّةِ أَبْعِرَةٍ ابْتَاعَهُنَّ حِينَئِذٍ مِنْ سَعْدٍ ـ فَانْطَلِقْ بِهِنَّ إِلَى أَصْحَابِكَ فَقُلْ إِنَّ اللَّهَ ـ أَوْ قَالَ إِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ يَحْمِلُكُمْ عَلَى هَؤُلاَءِ فَارْكَبُوهُنَّ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ فَانْطَلَقْتُ إِلَيْهِمْ بِهِنَّ، فَقُلْتُ إِنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَحْمِلُكُمْ عَلَى هَؤُلاَءِ وَلَكِنِّي وَاللَّهِ لاَ أَدَعُكُمْ حَتَّى يَنْطَلِقَ مَعِي بَعْضُكُمْ إِلَى مَنْ سَمِعَ مَقَالَةَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لاَ تَظُنُّوا أَنِّي حَدَّثْتُكُمْ شَيْئًا لَمْ يَقُلْهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالُوا لِي إِنَّكَ عِنْدَنَا لَمُصَدَّقٌ، وَلَنَفْعَلَنَّ مَا أَحْبَبْتَ‏.‏ فَانْطَلَقَ أَبُو مُوسَى بِنَفَرٍ مِنْهُمْ حَتَّى أَتَوُا الَّذِينَ سَمِعُوا قَوْلَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَنْعَهُ إِيَّاهُمْ، ثُمَّ إِعْطَاءَهُمْ بَعْدُ، فَحَدَّثُوهُمْ بِمِثْلِ مَا حَدَّثَهُمْ بِهِ أَبُو مُوسَى‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4415]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Musa: My Companions sent me to Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) to ask him for some animals to ride on as they were accompanying him in the army of Al-Usra, and that was the Ghazwa (Battle) of Tabuk, I said, &quot;O Allah&#x27;s Prophet! My companions have sent me to you to provide them with means of transportation.&quot; He said, &quot;By Allah! I will not make you ride anything.&quot; It happened that when I reached him, he was in an angry mood, and I didn&#x27;t notice it. So I returned in a sad mood because of the refusal of the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and for the fear that the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) might have become &#x27;angry with me. So I returned to my companions and informed them of what the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) had said. Only a short while had passed when I heard Bilal calling, &quot;O `Abdullah bin Qais!&quot; I replied to his call. Bilal said, &quot;Respond to Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) who is calling you.&quot; When I went to him (i.e. the Prophet), he said, &quot;Take these two camels tied together and also these two camels tied together,&quot;&#x27; referring to six camels he had brought them from Sa`d at that time. The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) added, &quot;Take them to your companions and say, &#x27;Allah (or Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) ) allows you to ride on these,&#x27; so ride on them.&quot; So I took those camels to them and said, &quot;The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) allows you to ride on these (camels) but by Allah, I will not leave you till some of you proceed with me to somebody who heard the statement of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him). Do not think that I narrate to you a thing which Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) has not said.&quot; They said to me, &quot;We consider you truthful, and we will do what you like.&quot; The sub-narrator added: So Abu Musa proceeded along with some of them till they came to those who have heard the statement of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) wherein he denied them (some animals to ride on) and (his statement) whereby he gave them the same. So these people told them the same information as Abu Musa had told them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4415]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मुझ से मुहम्मद बिन आला ने बयां किया और उन से अबू मूसा ऐश&#x27;आरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने बयां किया कि मुझे मेरे साथियों ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की खिदमत में भेजा के मैं आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से उन के लिए सवारी के जानवरों की दरख्वास्त करून. वह लोग आप के साथ जैश-इ-ुसरत (यानि ग़ज़वा-इ-तबूक) में शरीक होना चाहते थे. मैंने अर्ज़ किया: या रसूल अल्लाह! मेरे साथियों ने मुझे आप की खिदमत में भेजा है ताकि आप उन के लिए सवारी के जानवरों का इंतज़ाम करा दें. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अल्लाह की क़सम! मैं तुम को सवारी के जानवर नहीं दे सकता. मैं जब आप की खिदमत में हाज़िर हुआ था तो आप गुस्से में थे और मैं इसे मालूम न कर सका था. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के इंकार से मैं बहुत ग़मगीन वापस हुआ. यह खौफ भी था कि कहीं आप सवारी मांगने की वजह से खफा न हो गए हों. मैं अपने साथियों के पास आया और उन्हें नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के इरशाद की खबर दी लेकिन अभी कुछ ज़्यादा वक़्त नहीं गुज़रा था के मैं ने बिलाल (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) की आवाज़ सुनी अब्दुल्लाह बिन क़ैस! मैंने जवाब दिया तो उन्हों ने कहा कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) तुम्हें बुला रहे हैं. मैं आप की खिदमत में हाज़िर हुआ तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के यह दो जोड़े और यह दो जोड़े ऊँट के ले जाओ. आप ने छे ऊँट इनायत फरमाए. इन ऊंटों को आप ने उसी वक़्त साद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से खरीदा था और फ़रमाया के उन्हें अपने साथियों को दे दो और उन्हें बताओ की अल्लाह त&#x27;आला ने या आप ने फ़रमाया के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने तुम्हारी सवारी के लिए इन्हें दिया है अपने साथियों के पास गया और उन से मैं ने कहा के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने तुम्हारी सवारी के लिए यह इनायत फरमाए हैं लेकिन अल्लाह की क़सम! के अब तुम्हें उन सहाबा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुम) के पास चलना पड़ेगा नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का इंकार फरमान सुना था नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के इरशाद के मुतालिक ग़लत बात कह दी थी. उन्हों ने कहा कि तुम्हारी सच्चाई में हमें कोई शुबा नहीं है लेकिन अगर आप का इस्रार है तो हम ऐसा भी कर लेंगे. अबू मूसा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) उन में से चाँद लोगों को ले कर उन सहाबा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुम) के पास आये जिन्होंने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का वह इरशाद सुना था के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पहले तो देने से इंकार किया था लेकिन फिर इनायत फ़रमाया. उन सहाबा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुम) ने भी इसी तरह हदीथ बयां की जिस तरह अबू मूसा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने उन से बयां की थी.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4415]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مجھ سے محمد بن علاء نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے ابواسامہ نے بیان کیا، ان سے برید بن عبداللہ بن ابی بردہ نے اور ان سے ابوموسیٰ اشعری رضی اللہ عنہ نے بیان کیا کہ مجھے میرے ساتھیوں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی خدمت میں بھیجا کہ میں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے ان کے لیے سواری کے جانوروں کی درخواست کروں۔ وہ لوگ آپ کے ساتھ جیش عسرت ( یعنی غزوہ تبوک ) میں شریک ہونا چاہتے تھے۔ میں نے عرض کیا: یا رسول اللہ! میرے ساتھیوں نے مجھے آپ کی خدمت میں بھیجا ہے تاکہ آپ ان کے لیے سواری کے جانوروں کا انتظام کرا دیں۔ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اللہ کی قسم! میں تم کو سواری کے جانور نہیں دے سکتا۔ میں جب آپ کی خدمت میں حاضر ہوا تھا تو آپ غصہ میں تھے اور میں اسے معلوم نہ کر سکا تھا۔ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے انکار سے میں بہت غمگین واپس ہوا۔ یہ خوف بھی تھا کہ کہیں آپ سواری مانگنے کی وجہ سے خفا نہ ہو گئے ہوں۔ میں اپنے ساتھیوں کے پاس آیا اور انہیں نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ارشاد کی خبر دی، لیکن ابھی کچھ زیادہ وقت نہیں گزرا تھا کہ میں نے بلال رضی اللہ عنہ کی آواز سنی، وہ پکار رہے تھے، اے عبداللہ بن قیس! میں نے جواب دیا تو انہوں نے کہا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم تمہیں بلا رہے ہیں۔ میں آپ کی خدمت میں حاضر ہوا تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ یہ دو جوڑے اور یہ دو جوڑے اونٹ کے لے جاؤ۔ آپ نے چھ اونٹ عنایت فرمائے۔ ان اونٹوں کو آپ نے اسی وقت سعد رضی اللہ عنہ سے خریدا تھا اور فرمایا کہ انہیں اپنے ساتھیوں کو دے دو اور انہیں بتاؤ کہ اللہ تعالیٰ نے یا آپ نے فرمایا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے تمہاری سواری کے لیے انہیں دیا ہے، ان پر سوار ہو جاؤ۔ میں ان اونٹوں کو لے کر اپنے ساتھیوں کے پاس گیا اور ان سے میں نے کہا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے تمہاری سواری کے لیے یہ عنایت فرمائے ہیں لیکن اللہ کی قسم! کہ اب تمہیں ان صحابہ رضی اللہ عنہم کے پاس چلنا پڑے گا، جنہوں نے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کا انکار فرمانا سنا تھا، کہیں تم یہ خیال نہ کر بیٹھو کہ میں نے تم سے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ارشاد کے متعلق غلط بات کہہ دی تھی۔ انہوں نے کہا کہ تمہاری سچائی میں ہمیں کوئی شبہ نہیں ہے لیکن اگر آپ کا اصرار ہے تو ہم ایسا بھی کر لیں گے۔ ابوموسیٰ رضی اللہ عنہ ان میں سے چند لوگوں کو لے کر ان صحابہ رضی اللہ عنہم کے پاس آئے جنہوں نے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کا وہ ارشاد سنا تھا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پہلے تو دینے سے انکار کیا تھا لیکن پھر عنایت فرمایا۔ ان صحابہ رضی اللہ عنہم نے بھی اسی طرح حدیث بیان کی جس طرح ابوموسیٰ رضی اللہ عنہ نے ان سے بیان کی تھی۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4415]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Mujh se Muhammad bin Ala ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Abu Usama ne bayan kiya, un se Bareed bin Abdullah bin Abi Burda ne aur un se Abu Musa Ash&#x27;ari (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne bayan kiya ke mujhe mere sathiyon ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki khidmat mein bheja ke main aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se un ke liye sawari ke janwaron ki darkhwast karoon. Woh log aap ke sath Jaish-e-Usrat (yani Ghazwa-e-Tabuk) mein shareek hona chahte the. Maine arz kiya: Ya Rasool Allah! Mere sathiyon ne mujhe aap ki khidmat mein bheja hai taake aap un ke liye sawari ke janwaron ka intezam kara dein. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Allah ki qasam! Main tum ko sawari ke janwar nahi de sakta. Main jab aap ki khidmat mein hazir hua tha to aap gusse mein the aur main ise maloom na kar saka tha. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke inkar se main bahut ghamgeen wapas hua. Yeh khauf bhi tha ke kahin aap sawari mangne ki wajah se khafa na ho gaye hon. Main apne sathiyon ke paas aaya aur unhein Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke irshad ki khabar di, lekin abhi kuch zyada waqt nahi guzra tha ke main ne Bilal (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki awaz suni, woh pukar rahe the, Aye Abdullah bin Qais! Maine jawab diya to unhon ne kaha ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) tumhein bula rahe hain. Main aap ki khidmat mein hazir hua to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke yeh do jode aur yeh do jode oont ke le jao. Aap ne chhe oont inayat farmaye. In oonton ko aap ne usi waqt Saad (Radi Allahu Anhu) se khareeda tha aur farmaya ke unhein apne sathiyon ko de do aur unhein batao ke Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne ya aap ne farmaya ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne tumhari sawari ke liye inhein diya hai, in par sawar ho jao. Main in oonton ko le kar apne sathiyon ke paas gaya aur un se main ne kaha ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne tumhari sawari ke liye yeh inayat farmaye hain lekin Allah ki qasam! Ke ab tumhein un sahaba (Radi Allahu Anhum) ke paas chalna padega, jinhone Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ka inkar farmana suna tha, kahin tum yeh khayal na kar baitho ke main ne tum se Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke irshad ke mutaliq ghalat baat keh di thi. Unhon ne kaha ke tumhari sachai mein humein koi shuba nahi hai lekin agar aap ka israr hai to hum aisa bhi kar lenge. Abu Musa (Radi Allahu Anhu) un mein se chand logon ko le kar un sahaba (Radi Allahu Anhum) ke paas aaye jinhone Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ka woh irshad suna tha ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pehle to dene se inkar kiya tha lekin phir inayat farmaya. Un sahaba (Radi Allahu Anhum) ne bhi isi tarah hadith bayan ki jis tarah Abu Musa (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne un se bayan ki thi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4415]</p>

## Narration 267

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4416)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Military Expeditions led by the Prophet (pbuh) (Al-Maghaazi)</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنِ الْحَكَمِ، عَنْ مُصْعَبِ بْنِ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم خَرَجَ إِلَى تَبُوكَ، وَاسْتَخْلَفَ عَلِيًّا فَقَالَ أَتُخَلِّفُنِي فِي الصِّبْيَانِ وَالنِّسَاءِ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَلاَ تَرْضَى أَنْ تَكُونَ مِنِّي بِمَنْزِلَةِ هَارُونَ مِنْ مُوسَى إِلاَّ أَنَّهُ لَيْسَ نَبِيٌّ بَعْدِي ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ وَقَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ عَنِ الْحَكَمِ سَمِعْتُ مُصْعَبًا‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Sa`d: Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) set out for Tabuk. appointing `Ali as his deputy (in Medina). `Ali said, &quot;Do you want to leave me with the children and women?&quot; The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Will you not be pleased that you will be to me like Aaron to Moses? But there will be no prophet after me</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से मुसद्दद बिन मुसरहद ने बयान किया, कहा हम से यह्या बिन समीर क़त्तान ने बयान किया, उन से शुबा ने, उन से हाकम बिन उतबा ने, उन से मुसअब बिन साद ने और उन से उन के वालिद ने के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ग़ज़वा-ए-तबूक के लिए तशरीफ़ ले गये तो अली (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को मदीना में अपना नाएब बनाया। अली (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने अर्ज़ किया के आप मुझे बच्चों और औरतों में छोड़ रहे हैं? नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया क्या तुम इस पर ख़ुश नहीं हो के मेरे लिए तुम ऐसे हो जैसे मूसा के लिए हारून थे। लेकिन फ़र्क़ यह है के मेरे बाद कोई नबी नहीं होगा। और अबू दाऊद तयलिसी ने इस हदीस को यूँ बयान किया के हम से शुबा ने बयान किया, उन से हाकम बिन उतबा ने और उन्होंने कहा मैं ने मुसअब से सुना।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے مسدد بن مسرہد نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے یحییٰ بن سمیر قفان نے بیان کیا، ان سے شعبہ نے، ان سے حکم بن عتبہ نے، ان سے مصعب بن سعد نے اور ان سے ان کے والد نے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم غزوہ تبوک کے لیے تشریف لے گئے تو علی رضی اللہ عنہ کو مدینہ میں اپنا نائب بنایا۔ علی رضی اللہ عنہ نے عرض کیا کہ آپ مجھے بچوں اور عورتوں میں چھوڑے جا رہے ہیں؟ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کیا تم اس پر خوش نہیں ہو کہ میرے لیے تم ایسے ہو جیسے موسیٰ کے لیے ہارون تھے۔ لیکن فرق یہ ہے کہ میرے بعد کوئی نبی نہیں ہو گا۔ اور ابوداؤد طیالسی نے اس حدیث کو یوں بیان کیا کہ ہم سے شعبہ نے بیان کیا، ان سے حکم بن عتبہ نے اور انہوں نے کہا میں نے مصعب سے سنا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Musaddad bin Musarhad ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Yahya bin Sameer Qattan ne bayan kiya, un se Shu&#x27;ba ne, un se Hakam bin Utba ne, un se Mus&#x27;ab bin Saad ne aur un se un ke walid ne ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Ghazwa-e-Tabuk ke liye tashreef le gaye to Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko Madina mein apna naib banaya. Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne arz kiya ke aap mujhe bachon aur aurton mein chhod rahe hain? Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya kya tum is par khush nahi ho ke mere liye tum aise ho jaise Musa ke liye Haroon the. Lekin farq yeh hai ke mere baad koi Nabi nahi hoga. Aur Abu Dawood Tayalisi ne is hadith ko yun bayan kiya ke hum se Shu&#x27;ba ne bayan kiya, un se Hakam bin Utba ne aur unhon ne kaha main ne Mus&#x27;ab se suna.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4416]</p>

## Narration 268

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4417)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Military Expeditions led by the Prophet (pbuh) (Al-Maghaazi)</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَكْرٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ عَطَاءً، يُخْبِرُ قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي صَفْوَانُ بْنُ يَعْلَى بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، قَالَ غَزَوْتُ مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْعُسْرَةَ قَالَ كَانَ يَعْلَى يَقُولُ تِلْكَ الْغَزْوَةُ أَوْثَقُ أَعْمَالِي عِنْدِي‏.‏ قَالَ عَطَاءٌ فَقَالَ صَفْوَانُ قَالَ يَعْلَى فَكَانَ لِي أَجِيرٌ فَقَاتَلَ إِنْسَانًا فَعَضَّ أَحَدُهُمَا يَدَ الآخَرِ، قَالَ عَطَاءٌ فَلَقَدْ أَخْبَرَنِي صَفْوَانُ أَيُّهُمَا عَضَّ الآخَرَ فَنَسِيتُهُ، قَالَ فَانْتَزَعَ الْمَعْضُوضُ يَدَهُ مِنْ فِي الْعَاضِّ، فَانْتَزَعَ إِحْدَى ثَنِيَّتَيْهِ، فَأَتَيَا النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَأَهْدَرَ ثَنِيَّتَهُ‏.‏ قَالَ عَطَاءٌ وَحَسِبْتُ أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ أَفَيَدَعُ يَدَهُ فِي فِيكَ تَقْضَمُهَا، كَأَنَّهَا فِي فِي فَحْلٍ يَقْضَمُهَا ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4417]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4417" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Safwan bin Ya`la bin Umaiya: that his father said, &quot;I participated in Al-Usra (i.e. Tabuk) along with the Prophet.&quot; Ya`la added, &quot;(My participation in) that Ghazwa was the best of my deeds to me.&quot; Ya`la said, &quot;I had a laborer who quarrelled with somebody, and one of the two bit the hand of the other (`Ata&#x27;, the sub-narrator, said, &quot;Safwan told me who bit whom but I forgot it&quot;), and the one who was bitten, pulled his hand out of the mouth of the biter, so one of the incisors of the biter was broken. So we came to the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and he considered the biter&#x27;s claim as invalid (i.e. the biter did not get a recompense for his broken incisor). The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Should he leave his hand in your mouth so that you might snap it as if it were in the mouth of a male camel to snap it?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4417]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4417" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से उबैदुल्लाह बिन सईद ने बयान किया, कहा हम से मुहम्मद बिन बक्र ने बयान किया, कहा हम को इब्ने-जुरैज ने ख़बर दी, कहा के मैं ने अत्ता से सुना, उन्होंने ख़बर देते हुए कहा के मुझे सफ़वान बिन यअला बिन उमैया ने ख़बर दी और उन से उन के वालिद ने बयान किया के मैं नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ ग़ज़वा-ए-उस्रत में शरीक था। बयान किया के यअला (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) कहा करते थे के मुझे अपने तमाम अमलों में इसी पर सब से ज़्यादा भरोसा है। अत्ता ने बयान किया, उन से सफ़वान ने बयान किया के यअला (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा, मैं ने एक मज़दूर भी अपने साथ ले लिया था। वोह एक शख़्स से लड़ पड़ा और एक ने दूसरे का हाथ दाँत से काटा। अत्ता ने बयान किया के मुझे सफ़वान (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने ख़बर दी के उन दोनों में से किस ने अपने मुक़ाबिल का हाथ काटा था, यह मुझे याद नहीं है। बहरहाल जिस का हाथ काटा गया था उस ने अपना हाथ काटने वाले के मुँह से जो खींचा तो काटने वाले के आगे का एक दाँत भी साथ चला आया। वोह दोनों नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की खिदमत में हाज़िर हुए तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने दाँत के टूटने पर कोई क़िसास नहीं दिलवाया। अत्ता ने बयान किया मेरा ख़याल है के उन्होंने यह भी बयान किया के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया फिर क्या वोह तेरे मुँह में अपना हाथ रहने देता ताके तू उसे ऊँट की तरह चबा जाता।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4417]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے عبیداللہ بن سعید نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے محمد بن بکر نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم کو ابن جریج نے خبر دی، کہا کہ میں نے عطاء سے سنا، انہوں نے خبر دیتے ہوئے کہا کہ مجھے صفوان بن یعلٰی بن امیہ نے خبر دی اور ان سے ان کے والد نے بیان کیا کہ میں نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ غزوہ عسرت میں شریک تھا۔ بیان کیا کہ یعلٰی رضی اللہ عنہ کہا کرتے تھے کہ مجھے اپنے تمام عملوں میں اسی پر سب سے زیادہ بھروسہ ہے۔ عطاء نے بیان کیا، ان سے صفوان نے بیان کیا کہ یعلٰی رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہا، میں نے ایک مزدور بھی اپنے ساتھ لے لیا تھا۔ وہ ایک شخص سے لڑ پڑا اور ایک نے دوسرے کا ہاتھ دانت سے کاٹا۔ عطاء نے بیان کیا کہ مجھے صفوان رضی اللہ عنہ نے خبر دی کہ ان دونوں میں سے کس نے اپنے مقابل کا ہاتھ کاٹا تھا، یہ مجھے یاد نہیں ہے۔ بہرحال جس کا ہاتھ کاٹا گیا تھا اس نے اپنا ہاتھ کاٹنے والے کے منہ سے جو کھینچا تو کاٹنے والے کے آگے کا ایک دانت بھی ساتھ چلا آیا۔ وہ دونوں نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی خدمت میں حاضر ہوئے تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے دانت کے ٹوٹنے پر کوئی قصاص نہیں دلوایا۔ عطاء نے بیان کیا میرا خیال ہے کہ انہوں نے یہ بھی بیان کیا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا پھر کیا وہ تیرے منہ میں اپنا ہاتھ رہنے دیتا تاکہ تو اسے اونٹ کی طرح چبا جاتا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4417]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4417" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Ubaidullah bin Saeed ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Muhammad bin Bakr ne bayan kiya, kaha hum ko Ibn-e-Jurayj ne khabar di, kaha ke main ne Atta se suna, unhon ne khabar dete hue kaha ke mujhe Safwan bin Ya&#x27;la bin Umayya ne khabar di aur un se un ke walid ne bayan kiya ke main Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath Ghazwa-e-Usrat mein shareek tha. Bayan kiya ke Ya&#x27;la (Radi Allahu Anhu) kaha karte the ke mujhe apne tamam amlon mein isi par sab se zyada bharosa hai. Atta ne bayan kiya, un se Safwan ne bayan kiya ke Ya&#x27;la (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha, main ne ek mazdoor bhi apne sath le liya tha. Woh ek shakhs se lad pada aur ek ne dusre ka hath daant se kaata. Atta ne bayan kiya ke mujhe Safwan (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne khabar di ke un dono mein se kis ne apne muqabil ka hath kaata tha, yeh mujhe yaad nahi hai. Baharhal jis ka hath kaata gaya tha us ne apna hath kaatne wale ke munh se jo kheencha to kaatne wale ke aage ka ek daant bhi sath chala aaya. Woh dono Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki khidmat mein hazir hue to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne daant ke tootne par koi qisas nahi dilwaya. Atta ne bayan kiya mera khayal hai ke unhon ne yeh bhi bayan kiya ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya phir kya woh tere munh mein apna hath rehne deta taake tu use oont ki tarah chaba jaata.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4417]</p>

## Narration 269

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4418)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Military Expeditions led by the Prophet (pbuh) (Al-Maghaazi)</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ بُكَيْرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا اللَّيْثُ، عَنْ عُقَيْلٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ كَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ كَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ ـ وَكَانَ قَائِدَ كَعْبٍ مِنْ بَنِيهِ حِينَ عَمِيَ ـ قَالَ سَمِعْتُ كَعْبَ بْنَ مَالِكٍ، يُحَدِّثُ حِينَ تَخَلَّفَ عَنْ قِصَّةِ، تَبُوكَ قَالَ كَعْبٌ لَمْ أَتَخَلَّفْ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي غَزْوَةٍ غَزَاهَا إِلاَّ فِي غَزْوَةِ تَبُوكَ، غَيْرَ أَنِّي كُنْتُ تَخَلَّفْتُ فِي غَزْوَةِ بَدْرٍ، وَلَمْ يُعَاتِبْ أَحَدًا تَخَلَّفَ، عَنْهَا إِنَّمَا خَرَجَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُرِيدُ عِيرَ قُرَيْشٍ، حَتَّى جَمَعَ اللَّهُ بَيْنَهُمْ وَبَيْنَ عَدُوِّهِمْ عَلَى غَيْرِ مِيعَادٍ وَلَقَدْ شَهِدْتُ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لَيْلَةَ الْعَقَبَةِ حِينَ تَوَاثَقْنَا عَلَى الإِسْلاَمِ، وَمَا أُحِبُّ أَنَّ لِي بِهَا مَشْهَدَ بَدْرٍ، وَإِنْ كَانَتْ بَدْرٌ أَذْكَرَ فِي النَّاسِ مِنْهَا، كَانَ مِنْ خَبَرِي أَنِّي لَمْ أَكُنْ قَطُّ أَقْوَى وَلاَ أَيْسَرَ حِينَ تَخَلَّفْتُ عَنْهُ فِي تِلْكَ الْغَزْوَةِ، وَاللَّهِ مَا اجْتَمَعَتْ عِنْدِي قَبْلَهُ رَاحِلَتَانِ قَطُّ حَتَّى جَمَعْتُهُمَا فِي تِلْكَ الْغَزْوَةِ، وَلَمْ يَكُنْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُرِيدُ غَزْوَةً إِلاَّ وَرَّى بِغَيْرِهَا، حَتَّى كَانَتْ تِلْكَ الْغَزْوَةُ، غَزَاهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي حَرٍّ شَدِيدٍ، وَاسْتَقْبَلَ سَفَرًا بَعِيدًا وَمَفَازًا وَعَدُوًّا كَثِيرًا، فَجَلَّى لِلْمُسْلِمِينَ أَمْرَهُمْ لِيَتَأَهَّبُوا أُهْبَةَ غَزْوِهِمْ، فَأَخْبَرَهُمْ بِوَجْهِهِ الَّذِي يُرِيدُ، وَالْمُسْلِمُونَ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَثِيرٌ، وَلاَ يَجْمَعُهُمْ كِتَابٌ حَافِظٌ ـ يُرِيدُ الدِّيوَانَ ـ قَالَ كَعْبٌ فَمَا رَجُلٌ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَتَغَيَّبَ إِلاَّ ظَنَّ أَنْ سَيَخْفَى لَهُ مَا لَمْ يَنْزِلْ فِيهِ وَحْىُ اللَّهِ، وَغَزَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم تِلْكَ الْغَزْوَةَ حِينَ طَابَتِ الثِّمَارُ وَالظِّلاَلُ، وَتَجَهَّزَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَالْمُسْلِمُونَ مَعَهُ، فَطَفِقْتُ أَغْدُو لِكَىْ أَتَجَهَّزَ مَعَهُمْ فَأَرْجِعُ وَلَمْ أَقْضِ شَيْئًا، فَأَقُولُ فِي نَفْسِي أَنَا قَادِرٌ عَلَيْهِ‏.‏ فَلَمْ يَزَلْ يَتَمَادَى بِي حَتَّى اشْتَدَّ بِالنَّاسِ الْجِدُّ، فَأَصْبَحَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَالْمُسْلِمُونَ مَعَهُ وَلَمْ أَقْضِ مِنْ جَهَازِي شَيْئًا، فَقُلْتُ أَتَجَهَّزُ بَعْدَهُ بِيَوْمٍ أَوْ يَوْمَيْنِ ثُمَّ أَلْحَقُهُمْ، فَغَدَوْتُ بَعْدَ أَنْ فَصَلُوا لأَتَجَهَّزَ، فَرَجَعْتُ وَلَمْ أَقْضِ شَيْئًا، ثُمَّ غَدَوْتُ ثُمَّ رَجَعْتُ وَلَمْ أَقْضِ شَيْئًا، فَلَمْ يَزَلْ بِي حَتَّى أَسْرَعُوا وَتَفَارَطَ الْغَزْوُ، وَهَمَمْتُ أَنْ أَرْتَحِلَ فَأُدْرِكَهُمْ، وَلَيْتَنِي فَعَلْتُ، فَلَمْ يُقَدَّرْ لِي ذَلِكَ، فَكُنْتُ إِذَا خَرَجْتُ فِي النَّاسِ بَعْدَ خُرُوجِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَطُفْتُ فِيهِمْ، أَحْزَنَنِي أَنِّي لاَ أَرَى إِلاَّ رَجُلاً مَغْمُوصًا عَلَيْهِ النِّفَاقُ أَوْ رَجُلاً مِمَّنْ عَذَرَ اللَّهُ مِنَ الضُّعَفَاءِ، وَلَمْ يَذْكُرْنِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَتَّى بَلَغَ تَبُوكَ، فَقَالَ وَهْوَ جَالِسٌ فِي الْقَوْمِ بِتَبُوكَ ‏&quot;‏ مَا فَعَلَ كَعْبٌ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَجُلٌ مِنْ بَنِي سَلِمَةَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، حَبَسَهُ بُرْدَاهُ وَنَظَرُهُ فِي عِطْفِهِ‏.‏ فَقَالَ مُعَاذُ بْنُ جَبَلٍ بِئْسَ مَا قُلْتَ، وَاللَّهِ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، مَا عَلِمْنَا عَلَيْهِ إِلاَّ خَيْرًا‏.‏ فَسَكَتَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏.‏ قَالَ كَعْبُ بْنُ مَالِكٍ فَلَمَّا بَلَغَنِي أَنَّهُ تَوَجَّهَ قَافِلاً حَضَرَنِي هَمِّي، وَطَفِقْتُ أَتَذَكَّرُ الْكَذِبَ وَأَقُولُ بِمَاذَا أَخْرُجُ مِنْ سَخَطِهِ غَدًا وَاسْتَعَنْتُ عَلَى ذَلِكَ بِكُلِّ ذِي رَأْىٍ مِنْ أَهْلِي، فَلَمَّا قِيلَ إِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَدْ أَظَلَّ قَادِمًا زَاحَ عَنِّي الْبَاطِلُ، وَعَرَفْتُ أَنِّي لَنْ أَخْرُجَ مِنْهُ أَبَدًا بِشَىْءٍ فِيهِ كَذِبٌ، فَأَجْمَعْتُ صِدْقَهُ، وَأَصْبَحَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَادِمًا، وَكَانَ إِذَا قَدِمَ مِنْ سَفَرٍ بَدَأَ بِالْمَسْجِدِ فَيَرْكَعُ فِيهِ رَكْعَتَيْنِ ثُمَّ جَلَسَ لِلنَّاسِ، فَلَمَّا فَعَلَ ذَلِكَ جَاءَهُ الْمُخَلَّفُونَ، فَطَفِقُوا يَعْتَذِرُونَ إِلَيْهِ، وَيَحْلِفُونَ لَهُ، وَكَانُوا بِضْعَةً وَثَمَانِينَ رَجُلاً فَقَبِلَ مِنْهُمْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلاَنِيَتَهُمْ، وَبَايَعَهُمْ وَاسْتَغْفَرَ لَهُمْ، وَوَكَلَ سَرَائِرَهُمْ إِلَى اللَّهِ، فَجِئْتُهُ فَلَمَّا سَلَّمْتُ عَلَيْهِ تَبَسَّمَ تَبَسُّمَ الْمُغْضَبِ، ثُمَّ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ تَعَالَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ فَجِئْتُ أَمْشِي حَتَّى جَلَسْتُ بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ، فَقَالَ لِي ‏&quot;‏ مَا خَلَّفَكَ أَلَمْ تَكُنْ قَدِ ابْتَعْتَ ظَهْرَكَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ بَلَى، إِنِّي وَاللَّهِ لَوْ جَلَسْتُ عِنْدَ غَيْرِكَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الدُّنْيَا، لَرَأَيْتُ أَنْ سَأَخْرُجُ مِنْ سَخَطِهِ بِعُذْرٍ، وَلَقَدْ أُعْطِيتُ جَدَلاً، وَلَكِنِّي وَاللَّهِ لَقَدْ عَلِمْتُ لَئِنْ حَدَّثْتُكَ الْيَوْمَ حَدِيثَ كَذِبٍ تَرْضَى بِهِ عَنِّي لَيُوشِكَنَّ اللَّهُ أَنْ يُسْخِطَكَ عَلَىَّ، وَلَئِنْ حَدَّثْتُكَ حَدِيثَ صِدْقٍ تَجِدُ عَلَىَّ فِيهِ إِنِّي لأَرْجُو فِيهِ عَفْوَ اللَّهِ، لاَ وَاللَّهِ مَا كَانَ لِي مِنْ عُذْرٍ، وَاللَّهِ مَا كُنْتُ قَطُّ أَقْوَى وَلاَ أَيْسَرَ مِنِّي حِينَ تَخَلَّفْتُ عَنْكَ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَمَّا هَذَا فَقَدْ صَدَقَ، فَقُمْ حَتَّى يَقْضِيَ اللَّهُ فِيكَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ فَقُمْتُ وَثَارَ رِجَالٌ مِنْ بَنِي سَلِمَةَ فَاتَّبَعُونِي، فَقَالُوا لِي وَاللَّهِ مَا عَلِمْنَاكَ كُنْتَ أَذْنَبْتَ ذَنْبًا قَبْلَ هَذَا، وَلَقَدْ عَجَزْتَ أَنْ لاَ تَكُونَ اعْتَذَرْتَ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِمَا اعْتَذَرَ إِلَيْهِ الْمُتَخَلِّفُونَ، قَدْ كَانَ كَافِيَكَ ذَنْبَكَ اسْتِغْفَارُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لَكَ، فَوَاللَّهِ مَا زَالُوا يُؤَنِّبُونِي حَتَّى أَرَدْتُ أَنْ أَرْجِعَ فَأُكَذِّبُ نَفْسِي، ثُمَّ قُلْتُ لَهُمْ هَلْ لَقِيَ هَذَا مَعِي أَحَدٌ قَالُوا نَعَمْ، رَجُلاَنِ قَالاَ مِثْلَ مَا قُلْتَ، فَقِيلَ لَهُمَا مِثْلُ مَا قِيلَ لَكَ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ مَنْ هُمَا قَالُوا مُرَارَةُ بْنُ الرَّبِيعِ الْعَمْرِيُّ وَهِلاَلُ بْنُ أُمَيَّةَ الْوَاقِفِيُّ‏.‏ فَذَكَرُوا لِي رَجُلَيْنِ صَالِحَيْنِ قَدْ شَهِدَا بَدْرًا فِيهِمَا إِسْوَةٌ، فَمَضَيْتُ حِينَ ذَكَرُوهُمَا لِي، وَنَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْمُسْلِمِينَ عَنْ كَلاَمِنَا أَيُّهَا الثَّلاَثَةُ مِنْ بَيْنِ مَنْ تَخَلَّفَ عَنْهُ، فَاجْتَنَبَنَا النَّاسُ وَتَغَيَّرُوا لَنَا حَتَّى تَنَكَّرَتْ فِي نَفْسِي الأَرْضُ، فَمَا هِيَ الَّتِي أَعْرِفُ، فَلَبِثْنَا عَلَى ذَلِكَ خَمْسِينَ لَيْلَةً، فَأَمَّا صَاحِبَاىَ فَاسْتَكَانَا وَقَعَدَا فِي بُيُوتِهِمَا يَبْكِيَانِ، وَأَمَّا أَنَا فَكُنْتُ أَشَبَّ الْقَوْمِ وَأَجْلَدَهُمْ، فَكُنْتُ أَخْرُجُ فَأَشْهَدُ الصَّلاَةَ مَعَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ وَأَطُوفُ فِي الأَسْوَاقِ، وَلاَ يُكَلِّمُنِي أَحَدٌ، وَآتِي رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَأُسَلِّمُ عَلَيْهِ وَهْوَ فِي مَجْلِسِهِ بَعْدَ الصَّلاَةِ، فَأَقُولُ فِي نَفْسِي هَلْ حَرَّكَ شَفَتَيْهِ بِرَدِّ السَّلاَمِ عَلَىَّ أَمْ لاَ ثُمَّ أُصَلِّي قَرِيبًا مِنْهُ فَأُسَارِقُهُ النَّظَرَ، فَإِذَا أَقْبَلْتُ عَلَى صَلاَتِي أَقْبَلَ إِلَىَّ، وَإِذَا الْتَفَتُّ نَحْوَهُ أَعْرَضَ عَنِّي، حَتَّى إِذَا طَالَ عَلَىَّ ذَلِكَ مِنْ جَفْوَةِ النَّاسِ مَشَيْتُ حَتَّى تَسَوَّرْتُ جِدَارَ حَائِطِ أَبِي قَتَادَةَ وَهْوَ ابْنُ عَمِّي وَأَحَبُّ النَّاسِ إِلَىَّ، فَسَلَّمْتُ عَلَيْهِ، فَوَاللَّهِ مَا رَدَّ عَلَىَّ السَّلاَمَ، فَقُلْتُ يَا أَبَا قَتَادَةَ، أَنْشُدُكَ بِاللَّهِ هَلْ تَعْلَمُنِي أُحِبُّ اللَّهَ وَرَسُولَهُ فَسَكَتَ، فَعُدْتُ لَهُ فَنَشَدْتُهُ فَسَكَتَ، فَعُدْتُ لَهُ فَنَشَدْتُهُ‏.‏ فَقَالَ اللَّهُ وَرَسُولُهُ أَعْلَمُ‏.‏ فَفَاضَتْ عَيْنَاىَ وَتَوَلَّيْتُ حَتَّى تَسَوَّرْتُ الْجِدَارَ، قَالَ فَبَيْنَا أَنَا أَمْشِي بِسُوقِ الْمَدِينَةِ إِذَا نَبَطِيٌّ مِنْ أَنْبَاطِ أَهْلِ الشَّأْمِ مِمَّنْ قَدِمَ بِالطَّعَامِ يَبِيعُهُ بِالْمَدِينَةِ يَقُولُ مَنْ يَدُلُّ عَلَى كَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ فَطَفِقَ النَّاسُ يُشِيرُونَ لَهُ، حَتَّى إِذَا جَاءَنِي دَفَعَ إِلَىَّ كِتَابًا مِنْ مَلِكِ غَسَّانَ، فَإِذَا فِيهِ أَمَّا بَعْدُ فَإِنَّهُ قَدْ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ صَاحِبَكَ قَدْ جَفَاكَ، وَلَمْ يَجْعَلْكَ اللَّهُ بِدَارِ هَوَانٍ وَلاَ مَضْيَعَةٍ، فَالْحَقْ بِنَا نُوَاسِكَ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ لَمَّا قَرَأْتُهَا وَهَذَا أَيْضًا مِنَ الْبَلاَءِ‏.‏ فَتَيَمَّمْتُ بِهَا التَّنُّورَ فَسَجَرْتُهُ بِهَا، حَتَّى إِذَا مَضَتْ أَرْبَعُونَ لَيْلَةً مِنَ الْخَمْسِينَ إِذَا رَسُولُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَأْتِينِي فَقَالَ إِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَأْمُرُكَ أَنْ تَعْتَزِلَ امْرَأَتَكَ فَقُلْتُ أُطَلِّقُهَا أَمْ مَاذَا أَفْعَلُ قَالَ لاَ بَلِ اعْتَزِلْهَا وَلاَ تَقْرَبْهَا‏.‏ وَأَرْسَلَ إِلَى صَاحِبَىَّ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ، فَقُلْتُ لاِمْرَأَتِي الْحَقِي بِأَهْلِكِ فَتَكُونِي عِنْدَهُمْ حَتَّى يَقْضِيَ اللَّهُ فِي هَذَا الأَمْرِ‏.‏ قَالَ كَعْبٌ فَجَاءَتِ امْرَأَةُ هِلاَلِ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَتْ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنَّ هِلاَلَ بْنَ أُمَيَّةَ شَيْخٌ ضَائِعٌ لَيْسَ لَهُ خَادِمٌ فَهَلْ تَكْرَهُ أَنْ أَخْدُمَهُ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ وَلَكِنْ لاَ يَقْرَبْكِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ قَالَتْ إِنَّهُ وَاللَّهِ مَا بِهِ حَرَكَةٌ إِلَى شَىْءٍ، وَاللَّهِ مَا زَالَ يَبْكِي مُنْذُ كَانَ مِنْ أَمْرِهِ مَا كَانَ إِلَى يَوْمِهِ هَذَا‏.‏ فَقَالَ لِي بَعْضُ أَهْلِي لَوِ اسْتَأْذَنْتَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي امْرَأَتِكَ كَمَا أَذِنَ لاِمْرَأَةِ هِلاَلِ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ أَنْ تَخْدُمَهُ فَقُلْتُ وَاللَّهِ لاَ أَسْتَأْذِنُ فِيهَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَمَا يُدْرِينِي مَا يَقُولُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا اسْتَأْذَنْتُهُ فِيهَا وَأَنَا رَجُلٌ شَابٌّ فَلَبِثْتُ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ عَشْرَ لَيَالٍ حَتَّى كَمَلَتْ لَنَا خَمْسُونَ لَيْلَةً مِنْ حِينِ نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ كَلاَمِنَا، فَلَمَّا صَلَّيْتُ صَلاَةَ الْفَجْرِ صُبْحَ خَمْسِينَ لَيْلَةً، وَأَنَا عَلَى ظَهْرِ بَيْتٍ مِنْ بُيُوتِنَا، فَبَيْنَا أَنَا جَالِسٌ عَلَى الْحَالِ الَّتِي ذَكَرَ اللَّهُ، قَدْ ضَاقَتْ عَلَىَّ نَفْسِي، وَضَاقَتْ عَلَىَّ الأَرْضُ بِمَا رَحُبَتْ، سَمِعْتُ صَوْتَ صَارِخٍ أَوْفَى عَلَى جَبَلِ سَلْعٍ بِأَعْلَى صَوْتِهِ يَا كَعْبُ بْنَ مَالِكٍ، أَبْشِرْ‏.‏ قَالَ فَخَرَرْتُ سَاجِدًا، وَعَرَفْتُ أَنْ قَدْ جَاءَ فَرَجٌ، وَآذَنَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِتَوْبَةِ اللَّهِ عَلَيْنَا حِينَ صَلَّى صَلاَةَ الْفَجْرِ، فَذَهَبَ النَّاسُ يُبَشِّرُونَنَا، وَذَهَبَ قِبَلَ صَاحِبَىَّ مُبَشِّرُونَ، وَرَكَضَ إِلَىَّ رَجُلٌ فَرَسًا، وَسَعَى سَاعٍ مِنْ أَسْلَمَ فَأَوْفَى عَلَى الْجَبَلِ وَكَانَ الصَّوْتُ أَسْرَعَ مِنَ الْفَرَسِ، فَلَمَّا جَاءَنِي الَّذِي سَمِعْتُ صَوْتَهُ يُبَشِّرُنِي نَزَعْتُ لَهُ ثَوْبَىَّ، فَكَسَوْتُهُ إِيَّاهُمَا بِبُشْرَاهُ، وَاللَّهِ مَا أَمْلِكُ غَيْرَهُمَا يَوْمَئِذٍ، وَاسْتَعَرْتُ ثَوْبَيْنِ فَلَبِسْتُهُمَا، وَانْطَلَقْتُ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَيَتَلَقَّانِي النَّاسُ فَوْجًا فَوْجًا يُهَنُّونِي بِالتَّوْبَةِ، يَقُولُونَ لِتَهْنِكَ تَوْبَةُ اللَّهِ عَلَيْكَ‏.‏ قَالَ كَعْبٌ حَتَّى دَخَلْتُ الْمَسْجِدَ، فَإِذَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم جَالِسٌ حَوْلَهُ النَّاسُ فَقَامَ إِلَىَّ طَلْحَةُ بْنُ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ يُهَرْوِلُ حَتَّى صَافَحَنِي وَهَنَّانِي، وَاللَّهِ مَا قَامَ إِلَىَّ رَجُلٌ مِنَ الْمُهَاجِرِينَ غَيْرُهُ، وَلاَ أَنْسَاهَا لِطَلْحَةَ، قَالَ كَعْبٌ فَلَمَّا سَلَّمْتُ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهْوَ يَبْرُقُ وَجْهُهُ مِنَ السُّرُورِ ‏&quot;‏ أَبْشِرْ بِخَيْرِ يَوْمٍ مَرَّ عَلَيْكَ مُنْذُ وَلَدَتْكَ أُمُّكَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ قَالَ قُلْتُ أَمِنْ عِنْدِكَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَمْ مِنْ عِنْدِ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ، بَلْ مِنْ عِنْدِ اللَّهِ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ وَكَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا سُرَّ اسْتَنَارَ وَجْهُهُ حَتَّى كَأَنَّهُ قِطْعَةُ قَمَرٍ، وَكُنَّا نَعْرِفُ ذَلِكَ مِنْهُ، فَلَمَّا جَلَسْتُ بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ قُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنَّ مِنْ تَوْبَتِي أَنْ أَنْخَلِعَ مِنْ مَالِي صَدَقَةً إِلَى اللَّهِ وَإِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ‏.‏ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَمْسِكْ عَلَيْكَ بَعْضَ مَالِكَ فَهُوَ خَيْرٌ لَكَ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏ قُلْتُ فَإِنِّي أُمْسِكُ سَهْمِي الَّذِي بِخَيْبَرَ، فَقُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنَّ اللَّهَ إِنَّمَا نَجَّانِي بِالصِّدْقِ، وَإِنَّ مِنْ تَوْبَتِي أَنْ لاَ أُحَدِّثَ إِلاَّ صِدْقًا مَا بَقِيتُ، فَوَاللَّهِ مَا أَعْلَمُ أَحَدًا مِنَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ أَبْلاَهُ اللَّهُ فِي صِدْقِ الْحَدِيثِ مُنْذُ ذَكَرْتُ ذَلِكَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَحْسَنَ مِمَّا أَبْلاَنِي، مَا تَعَمَّدْتُ مُنْذُ ذَكَرْتُ ذَلِكَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى يَوْمِي هَذَا كَذِبًا، وَإِنِّي لأَرْجُو أَنْ يَحْفَظَنِي اللَّهُ فِيمَا بَقِيتُ وَأَنْزَلَ اللَّهُ عَلَى رَسُولِهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏{‏لَقَدْ تَابَ اللَّهُ عَلَى النَّبِيِّ وَالْمُهَاجِرِينَ‏}‏ إِلَى قَوْلِهِ ‏{‏وَكُونُوا مَعَ الصَّادِقِينَ‏}‏ فَوَاللَّهِ مَا أَنْعَمَ اللَّهُ عَلَىَّ مِنْ نِعْمَةٍ قَطُّ بَعْدَ أَنْ هَدَانِي لِلإِسْلاَمِ أَعْظَمَ فِي نَفْسِي مِنْ صِدْقِي لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ لاَ أَكُونَ كَذَبْتُهُ، فَأَهْلِكَ كَمَا هَلَكَ الَّذِينَ كَذَبُوا، فَإِنَّ اللَّهَ قَالَ لِلَّذِينَ كَذَبُوا حِينَ أَنْزَلَ الْوَحْىَ شَرَّ مَا قَالَ لأَحَدٍ، فَقَالَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏سَيَحْلِفُونَ بِاللَّهِ لَكُمْ إِذَا انْقَلَبْتُمْ‏}‏ إِلَى قَوْلِهِ ‏{‏فَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لاَ يَرْضَى عَنِ الْقَوْمِ الْفَاسِقِينَ‏}‏‏.‏ قَالَ كَعْبٌ وَكُنَّا تَخَلَّفْنَا أَيُّهَا الثَّلاَثَةُ عَنْ أَمْرِ أُولَئِكَ الَّذِينَ قَبِلَ مِنْهُمْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حِينَ حَلَفُوا لَهُ، فَبَايَعَهُمْ وَاسْتَغْفَرَ لَهُمْ وَأَرْجَأَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمْرَنَا حَتَّى قَضَى اللَّهُ فِيهِ، فَبِذَلِكَ قَالَ اللَّهُ ‏{‏وَعَلَى الثَّلاَثَةِ الَّذِينَ خُلِّفُوا‏}‏ وَلَيْسَ الَّذِي ذَكَرَ اللَّهُ مِمَّا خُلِّفْنَا عَنِ الْغَزْوِ إِنَّمَا هُوَ تَخْلِيفُهُ إِيَّانَا وَإِرْجَاؤُهُ أَمْرَنَا عَمَّنْ حَلَفَ لَهُ وَاعْتَذَرَ إِلَيْهِ، فَقَبِلَ مِنْهُ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated `Abdullah bin Ka`b bin Malik: Who, from among Ka`b&#x27;s sons, was the guide of Ka`b when he became blind: I heard Ka`b bin Malik narrating the story of (the Ghazwa of) Tabuk in which he failed to take part. Ka`b said, &quot;I did not remain behind Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) in any Ghazwa that he fought except the Ghazwa of Tabuk, and I failed to take part in the Ghazwa of Badr, but Allah did not admonish anyone who had not participated in it, for in fact, Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) had gone out in search of the caravan of Quraish till Allah made them (i.e. the Muslims) and their enemy meet without any appointment. I witnessed the night of Al-`Aqaba (pledge) with Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) when we pledged for Islam, and I would not exchange it for the Badr battle although the Badr battle is more popular amongst the people than it (i.e. Al-`Aqaba pledge). As for my news (in this battle of Tabuk), I had never been stronger or wealthier than I was when I remained behind the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) in that Ghazwa. By Allah, never had I two she-camels before, but I had then at the time of this Ghazwa. Whenever Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) wanted to make a Ghazwa, he used to hide his intention by apparently referring to different Ghazwa till it was the time of that Ghazwa (of Tabuk) which Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) fought in severe heat, facing, a long journey, desert, and the great number of enemy. So the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) announced to the Muslims clearly (their destination) so that they might get prepared for their Ghazwa. So he informed them clearly of the destination he was going to. Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) was accompanied by a large number of Muslims who could not be listed in a book namely, a register.&quot; Ka`b added, &quot;Any man who intended to be absent would think that the matter would remain hidden unless Allah revealed it through Divine Revelation. So Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) fought that Ghazwa at the time when the fruits had ripened and the shade looked pleasant. Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and his companions prepared for the battle and I started to go out in order to get myself ready along with them, but I returned without doing anything. I would say to myself, &#x27;I can do that.&#x27; So I kept on delaying it every now and then till the people got ready and Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and the Muslims along with him departed, and I had not prepared anything for my departure, and I said, I will prepare myself (for departure) one or two days after him, and then join them.&#x27; In the morning following their departure, I went out to get myself ready but returned having done nothing. Then again in the next morning, I went out to get ready but returned without doing anything. Such was the case with me till they hurried away and the battle was missed (by me). Even then I intended to depart to take them over. I wish I had done so! But it was not in my luck. So, after the departure of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), whenever I went out and walked amongst the people (i.e, the remaining persons), it grieved me that I could see none around me, but one accused of hypocrisy or one of those weak men whom Allah had excused. Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) did not remember me till he reached Tabuk. So while he was sitting amongst the people in Tabuk, he said, &#x27;What did Ka`b do?&#x27; A man from Banu Salama said, &#x27;O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)! He has been stopped by his two Burdas (i.e. garments) and his looking at his own flanks with pride.&#x27; Then Mu`adh bin Jabal said, &#x27;What a bad thing you have said! By Allah! O Allah&#x27;s Apostle! We know nothing about him but good.&#x27; Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) kept silent.&quot; Ka`b bin Malik added, &quot;When I heard that he (i.e. the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) ) was on his way back to Medina. I got dipped in my concern, and began to think of false excuses, saying to myself, &#x27;How can I avoid his anger tomorrow?&#x27; And I took the advice of every wise member of my family in this matter. When it was said that Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him), had come near all the evil false excuses abandoned from my mind and I knew well that I could never come out of this problem by forging a false statement. Then I decided firmly to speak the truth. So Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) arrived in the morning, and whenever he returned from a journey., he used to visit the Mosque first of all and offer a two-rak`at prayer therein and then sit for the people. So when he had done all that (this time), those who had failed to join the battle (of Tabuk) came and started offering (false) excuses and taking oaths before him. They were something over eighty men; Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) accepted the excuses they had expressed, took their pledge of allegiance asked for Allah&#x27;s Forgiveness for them, and left the secrets of their hearts for Allah to judge. Then I came to him, and when I greeted him, he smiled a smile of an angry person and then said, &#x27;Come on.&#x27; So I came walking till I sat before him. He said to me, &#x27;What stopped you from joining us. Had you not purchased an animal For carrying you?&#x27; I answered, &quot;Yes, O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)! But by Allah, if I were sitting before any person from among the people of the world other than you, I would have avoided his anger with an excuse. By Allah, I have been bestowed with the power of speaking fluently and eloquently, but by Allah, I know well that if today I tell you a lie to seek your favor, Allah would surely make you angry with me in the near future, but if I tell you the truth, though you will get angry because of it, I hope for Allah&#x27;s Forgiveness. Really, by Allah, there was no excuse for me. By Allah, I had never been stronger or wealthier than I was when I remained behind you.&#x27; Then Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &#x27;As regards this man, he has surely told the truth. So get up till Allah decides your case.&#x27; I got up, and many men of Banu Salama followed me and said to me, &#x27;By Allah, we never witnessed you doing any sin before this. Surely, you failed to offer excuse to Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) as the others who did not join him, have offered. The prayer of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) to Allah to forgive you would have been sufficient for you.&#x27; By Allah, they continued blaming me so much that I intended to return (to the Prophet) and accuse myself of having told a lie, but I said to them, &#x27;Is there anybody else who has met the same fate as I have?&#x27; They replied, &#x27;Yes, there are two men who have said the same thing as you have, and to both of them was given the same order as given to you.&#x27; I said, &#x27;Who are they?&#x27; They replied, &#x27;Murara bin Ar-Rabi Al-Amri and Hilal bin Umaiya Al-Waqifi.&#x27; By that they mentioned to me two pious men who had attended the Ghazwa (Battle) of Badr, and in whom there was an example for me. So I did not change my mind when they mentioned them to me. Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) forbade all the Muslims to talk to us, the three aforesaid persons out of all those who had remained behind in that Ghazwa. So we kept away from the people and they changed their attitude towards us till the very land (where I lived) appeared strange to me as if I did not know it. We remained in that condition for fifty nights. As regards my two fellows, they remained in their houses and kept on weeping, but I was the youngest of them and the firmest of them, so I used to go out and witness the prayers along with the Muslims and roam about in the markets, but none would talk to me, and I would come to Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and greet him while he was sitting In his gathering after the prayer, and I would wonder whether the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) did move his lips in return to my greetings or not. Then I would offer my prayer near to him and look at him stealthily. When I was busy with my prayer, he would turn his face towards me, but when I turned my face to him, he would turn his face away from me. When this harsh attitude of the people lasted long, I walked till I scaled the wall of the garden of Abu Qatada who was my cousin and the dearest person to me, and I offered my greetings to him. By Allah, he did not return my greetings. I said, &#x27;O Abu Qatada! I beseech you by Allah! Do you know that I love Allah and His Apostle?&#x27; He kept quiet. I asked him again, beseeching him by Allah, but he remained silent. Then I asked him again in the Name of Allah. He said, &quot;Allah and His Apostle know it better.&#x27; Thereupon my eyes flowed with tears and I returned and jumped over the wall.&quot; Ka`b added, &quot;While I was walking in the market of Medina, suddenly I saw a Nabati (i.e. a Christian farmer) from the Nabatis of Sham who came to sell his grains in Medina, saying, &#x27;Who will lead me to Ka`b bin Malik?&#x27; The people began to point (me) out for him till he came to me and handed me a letter from the king of Ghassan in which the following was written: &quot;To proceed, I have been informed that your friend (i.e. the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) ) has treated you harshly. Anyhow, Allah does not let you live at a place where you feel inferior and your right is lost. So join us, and we will console you.&quot; When I read it, I said to myself, &#x27;This is also a sort of a test.&#x27; Then I took the letter to the oven and made a fire therein by burning it. When forty out of the fifty nights elapsed, behold ! There came to me the messenger of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and said, &#x27;Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) orders you to keep away from your wife,&#x27; I said, &#x27;Should I divorce her; or else what should I do?&#x27; He said, &#x27;No, only keep aloof from her and do not cohabit her.&#x27; The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) sent the same message to my two fellows. Then I said to my wife. &#x27;Go to your parents and remain with them till Allah gives His Verdict in this matter.&quot; Ka`b added, &quot;The wife of Hilal bin Umaiya came to Allah&#x27;s Apostle and said, &#x27;O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)! Hilal bin Umaiya is a helpless old man who has no servant to attend on him. Do you dislike that I should serve him? &#x27; He said, &#x27;No (you can serve him) but he should not come near you.&#x27; She said, &#x27;By Allah, he has no desire for anything. By, Allah, he has never ceased weeping since his case began till this day of his.&#x27; On that, some of my family members said to me, &#x27;Will you also ask Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) to permit your wife (to serve you) as he has permitted the wife of Hilal bin Umaiya to serve him?&#x27; I said, &#x27;By Allah, I will not ask the permission of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) regarding her, for I do not know What Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) would say if I asked him to permit her (to serve me) while I am a young man.&#x27; Then I remained in that state for ten more nights after that till the period of fifty nights was completed starting from the time when Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) prohibited the people from talking to us. When I had offered the Fajr prayer on the 50th morning on the roof of one of our houses and while I was sitting in the condition which Allah described (in the Qur&#x27;an) i.e. my very soul seemed straitened to me and even the earth seemed narrow to me for all its spaciousness, there I heard the voice of one who had ascended the mountain of Sala&#x27; calling with his loudest voice, &#x27;O Ka`b bin Malik! Be happy (by receiving good tidings).&#x27; I fell down in prostration before Allah, realizing that relief has come. Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) had announced the acceptance of our repentance by Allah when he had offered the Fajr prayer. The people then went out to congratulate us. Some bringers of good tidings went out to my two fellows, and a horseman came to me in haste, and a man of Banu Aslam came running and ascended the mountain and his voice was swifter than the horse. When he (i.e. the man) whose voice I had heard, came to me conveying the good tidings, I took off my garments and dressed him with them; and by Allah, I owned no other garments than them on that day. Then I borrowed two garments and wore them and went to Allah&#x27;s Apostle. The people started receiving me in batches, congratulating me on Allah&#x27;s Acceptance of my repentance, saying, &#x27;We congratulate you on Allah&#x27;s Acceptance of your repentance.&quot; Ka`b further said, &quot;When I entered the Mosque, I saw Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) sitting with the people around him. Talha bin Ubaidullah swiftly came to me, shook hands with me and congratulated me. By Allah, none of the Muhajirin (i.e. Emigrants) got up for me except him (i.e. Talha), and I will never forget this for Talha.&quot; Ka`b added, &quot;When I greeted Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) his face being bright with joy, he said, &quot;Be happy with the best day that you have got ever since your mother delivered you.&quot; Ka`b added, &quot;I said to the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) &#x27;Is this forgiveness from you or from Allah?&#x27; He said, &#x27;No, it is from Allah.&#x27; Whenever Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) became happy, his face would shine as if it were a piece of moon, and we all knew that characteristic of him. When I sat before him, I said, &#x27;O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)! Because of the acceptance of my repentance I will give up all my wealth as alms for the Sake of Allah and His Apostle. Allah&#x27;s Apostle said, &#x27;Keep some of your wealth, as it will be better for you.&#x27; I said, &#x27;So I will keep my share from Khaibar with me,&#x27; and added, &#x27;O Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him)! Allah has saved me for telling the truth; so it is a part of my repentance not to tell but the truth as long as I am alive. By Allah, I do not know anyone of the Muslims whom Allah has helped fortelling the truth more than me. Since I have mentioned that truth to Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) till today, I have never intended to tell a lie. I hope that Allah will also save me (from telling lies) the rest of my life. So Allah revealed to His Apostle the Verse:-- &quot;Verily, Allah has forgiven the Prophet, the Muhajirin (i.e. Emigrants (up to His Saying) And be with those who are true (in word and deed).&quot; (9.117-119) By Allah, Allah has never bestowed upon me, apart from His guiding me to Islam, a Greater blessing than the fact that I did not tell a lie to Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) which would have caused me to perish as those who have told a lie perished, for Allah described those who told lies with the worst description He ever attributed to anybody else. Allah said:-- &quot;They (i.e. the hypocrites) will swear by Allah to you when you return to them (up to His Saying) Certainly Allah is not pleased with the rebellious people-- &quot; (9.95-96) Ka`b added, &quot;We, the three persons, differed altogether from those whose excuses Allah&#x27;s Apostle accepted when they swore to him. He took their pledge of allegiance and asked Allah to forgive them, but Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) left our case pending till Allah gave His Judgment about it. As for that Allah said):-- And to the three (He did forgive also) who remained behind.&quot; (9.118) What Allah said (in this Verse) does not indicate our failure to take part in the Ghazwa, but it refers to the deferment of making a decision by the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) about our case in contrast to the case of those who had taken an oath before him and he excused them by accepting their excuses</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4418]</p>
